LP iteration in detail:
```
def lp_improve(f, max_iters, t=0.5):
    n=len(f); S=f.sum(); M=max(conv(f,f))? Use internal qraw? But LP M should be raw score? We'll use raw with f (sum not 1). Compute conv_raw via FFT or convolve. Since f values maybe O(1), sum n, M O(n). Use FFT safe. But for LP A coefficients f, RHS M. 
    For it:
       A sparse: rows L=2n-1, cols n, entries f[j] where i-j in [0,n-1]? A[i,j]=f[j] if 0≤i-j<n.
       res=linprog(-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=full(L,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
       if res.success:
          g=res.x
          sum_g=res.fun*-1
          if sum_g > S*(1+1e-6):
             g=scale(g,S)
             t_candidate in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]
             f_new=(1-t)*f+t*g
             score_new=score(f_new)
             if score_new<score_old: f=f_new; break?
    return f
```

def polish_sequence(p, budget_s=60.0, beta=80.0, verbose=True):
    n=p.size
    p=p.copy()
    p=np.clip(p,0,None); p/=p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=internal_score(best)
    p0=best.copy()
    # floor
    floor=1e-8
    p=(1-floor)*p + floor/n
    p/=p.sum()
    eta=0.35
    no_improve=0
    start=time.time()
    while time.time()-start < budget_s:
        q,maxq = convolve_sq(p)
        M=maxq
        if not finite: break
        score=2*n*M
        # softmax weights
        qx = beta*(q-M)/max? Use shifted = beta*(q-M)
        sx = np.exp(shifted)
        sx /= sx.sum()
        h = corr_h(p, sx)
        h = (h - h.mean())/(h.std()+1e-12)
        # maybe random jitter
        p_new = p * np.exp(-eta*h)
        p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 1e-12)
        p_new /= p_new.sum()
        score_new = internal_score_score_only(p_new)[1]? compute q? We already need q for p_new.
        if score_new < score: accept; eta=min(eta*1.1,0.9); no_improve=0
        else:
            # try smaller eta
            improved=False
            for e in [eta*0.5, eta*0.25]: ...
            if none: eta*=0.8; no_improve++
        if score_new < best_score: best=p_new...
        if no_improve>30: restart with noise from p0 or best
    return best, best_score
```

Let's refine LP details. Building sparse matrix:
```
from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix, csr_matrix
def build_conv_matrix(f):
    n=len(f); L=2*n-1
    A = lil_matrix((L,n))
    for i in range(L):
        j0=max(0,i-n+1); j1=min(n-1,i)
        A[i,j0:j1+1] = f[j0:j1+1]
    return A.tocsr()
```

- `make_two_step(n, split, high_low_ratio)`: Let k=min(max(1,int(n*split)),n-1). low value L = 1/(1 + ratio*(n-k)/k). Since sum =1 (normalized). H=ratio*L. p[0:k]=L; p[k:]=H. This normalizes sum1. If split >0.5? We can still. For ratio>1, low left, high right. If ratio<1, reversed. We'll sample split in [0.2,0.8], ratio in [0.5,2,3]. Since reversal same, ratio<1 corresponds high left low right (reversed of two-step with ratio>1 and split 1-s). But include.
- `make_blocks(n, nb, values)`: values shape nb, nonnegative. if sum=0 skip. Normalize mean1: v = v/v.mean(). Expand: reps = np.repeat(v, n//nb + ...). Easier: p = np.tile(v, reps) truncated; or `p = np.repeat(v, counts)` where counts = np.divide(n, nb, out=..., remaining). Then normalize p (sum not exactly 1 due value mean? If v mean1, sum ~n; p should be v/n to sum1. Since we want p_i = v_i/n (for equal blocks) gives sum ~1. If block lengths vary, p_i = v_i/n? Sum =∑ v_i * count_i / n. If counts not equal, normalize afterward. So p=np.repeat(v,counts)/n? Then normalize.
- Generate block values: For nb, patterns:
   * linear: v = 1 + a*(linspace(0,1,nb) -0.5) for a in [-1, -0.5,0.5,1,1.5,2]; clip 0; if all zero skip.
   * concave: v = 1 + a*np.sin(π*x)^? maybe.
   * exponential: v = exp(b*x) for b in [-2..2], normalize.
   * random lognormal: sorted? We'll generate random in a loop.
- `make_sine(n, m, amp, phase)`: x=np.arange(n)/n; F=1+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+phase); clip >=0; if sum>0 p=F/F.sum(). Ensure amp≤1 to keep nonneg theoretically but phase may make min negative; clip.
- `make_random_fourier(n, rng)`: K small; F=1+sum amp*sin(freq*2πx+phase); clip; normalize. Use rng.
- `make_small_n_interpolated(n_small_shape, n)`: linear interpolate to n. Use `np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), x0, y0)`, normalize.
- `downsample_sequence(a, n_small)`: if len large, maybe `np.interp` to small for LP? Or use block mean? Use `np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n_small), np.linspace(0,1,len(a)), a)`, normalize.

Helper `get_fft_len(n)`:
```
def get_fft_len(n):
    L = max(1, 2*int(n)-1)
    if HAVE_NEXT_FAST:
        try:
            return int(next_fast_len(L))
        except Exception:
            pass
    # power of two
    if L <= 1: return 1
    # use integer bit_length
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
```
Bit_length: `(L).bit_length()` gives bits for L; for next power >=L, if L is power of two, 1<<(L.bit_length()-1) maybe. Use:
```
bits = (L - 1).bit_length()
return 1 << bits
```
For L=8, (7).bit_length=3 ->8; L=9 ->(8).bit_length=4 ->16. Good.

`normalize_nonnegative(a, fallback_len=None)`:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    x=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1000.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
       return np.ones(max(1,x.size))/max(1,x.size)
    return x/s
```
`score_normalized(p)`:
```
def score_normalized(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    p=p/s
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    m=get_fft_len(n)
    fut=np.fft.rfft(p,m)
    q=np.fft.irfft(fut*fut,m)
    maxq=q[:2*n-1].max()
    if not np.isfinite(maxq) or maxq<0: maxq=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*maxq)
```
We might cache m? not necessary. But in polish, calling score_normalized recomputes m. We'll define `score_pre(p,m,n)` for precomputed.

`score_pre(p,m,n)` as above.

`conv_weights_pre(p, m, n)` returns q, maxq.

`compute_h_pre(p, sx, m, n)` returns h.

`smooth_weights(q, beta)`:
```
def smooth_weights(q, beta):
    M = float(q.max())
    if M <= 0:
        w=np.full(q.size,1.0/q.size)
    else:
        shifted=beta*(q-M)
        w=np.exp(shifted)
        wsum=w.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum==0:
            w=np.full(q.size,1.0/q.size)
        else:
            w/=wsum
    return w
```
Beta maybe 80. If q-M negative large, underflow ok.

`polish_sequence(p, budget_s, beta=80.0, name='')`:
Implementation careful with time. Use `time.monotonic()`.
```
def polish_sequence(p, budget_s, beta=80.0, name=''):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p=np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum(); if s<=: return uniform, inf
    p/=s
    n=p.size; m=get_fft_len(n)
    floor=1e-12
    p=(1.0-floor)*p + floor/n
    p/=p.sum()
    best=p.copy()
    best_q,best_max=conv_weights_pre(best,m,n)
    best_score=float(2*n*best_max)
    cur=p.copy()
    eta=0.35
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,budget_s)
    no_improve=0
    restarts=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       q,M=conv_weights_pre(cur,m,n)
       if not finite...
       score=2*n*M
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best=cur.copy(); no_improve=0
       else:
          # if current not better than best but we proceed
       sx=smooth_weights(q,beta)
       h=compute_h_pre(cur,sx,m,n)
       hmean=h.mean(); hstd=h.std(); if hstd<eps: h_unit=zeros
       else: h_unit=(h-hmean)/hstd
       # even if no improve, try eta sequence
       tried=[]
       improved=False
       # base eta
       for e in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
           p_new = cur * np.exp(-e*h_unit)
           p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 1e-15)
           sn=p_new.sum()
           if sn<=0: continue
           p_new/=sn
           sc=score_pre(p_new,m,n)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score:
               cur=p_new.copy()
               eta = min(max(e, 0.02), 1.5)*1.05
               improved=True
               break
       if not improved:
           eta *= 0.8
           no_improve +=1
       else:
           no_improve=0
       q,M=conv_weights_pre(cur,m,n)
       score=...
       if score < best_score: update
       if no_improve > 30:
           # restart around best
           noise=0.08 if restarts<5 else 0.15
           cur=best*(1-noise) + noise*(np.ones(n)/n)
           # add small random? Use rng_global.random(n)
           cur = cur + 0.01*rng_global.random(n)
           cur=np.maximum(cur,0); cur/=cur.sum()
           no_improve=0; eta=0.4; restarts+=1
           if restarts>10 and no_improve? maybe break after? We can continue.
       it+=1
       if it % 100 ==0: print progress
    # final best
    return best.copy(), best_score
```
Potential bug: If score improves but not beyond best, no_improve not reset. We can reset when current score < best_score? Already. If current quickly decreases but not below best, no matter. New best may occur later. Use `if score < best_score` update and reset. Good.

etas = (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25) if no_improve>3 else (eta,)
```

LP function:
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
    n=f.size; L=2*n-1
    # Use lil or coo
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    for i in range(L):
       j0=max(0,i-n+1); j1=min(n-1,i)
       rows.extend([i]*(j1-j0+1)); cols.extend(range(j0,j1+1)); data.append(f[j0:j1+1])
    A=scipy.sparse.csr_matrix(...)
```
Avoid Python loops? n small. But using `scipy.sparse.lil_matrix` easier:
```
from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix
A=lil_matrix((L,n))
for i in range(L):
    j0...; A[i,j0:j1+1] = f[j0:j1+1]
return A.tocsr()
```
Need import scipy.sparse if HAVE_LINPROG. Inside try.

`lp_polish(p, n_lp, budget_s, max_iters=5)`:
```
def lp_polish(p, n_lp, budget_s, max_iters=5):
    n=n_lp; p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
    if p.size != n: p=interpolate_to(p,n)
    f=p*n  # sum n
    m=get_fft_len(n)
    q,M=conv_weights_pre(p,m,n)
    best_score=float(2*n*M)
    best=p.copy()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       # recompute M with f norm? p changed maybe
       q,M=conv_weights_pre(p,m,n)
       M_raw=M*n*n  # since p sum1; f sum n, conv(f,f)=n^2 conv(p,p)
       f=p*n
       try: A=build_conv_matrix(f)
       except Exception: break
       b=np.full(2*n-1, M_raw)
       try: res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8})
       except Exception: break
       if not res.success: break
       g=np.clip(res.x,0,None)
       sum_g=float(g.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(sum_g) or sum_g <= n*(1+1e-7): break
       g = g * (n/sum_g)
       improved=False
       for t in (0.6,0.35,0.18):
          f_new=(1-t)*f + t*g
          if f_new.min()<0: f_new=np.maximum(f_new,0)
          p_new=f_new/n

          p_new/=psum

          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
             p=p_new.copy(); f=f_new
             best_score=sc; best=p_new.copy(); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          break
    return best.copy(), best_score

Let's detail low-dim optimizer code:
```
def ctrl_to_p(ctrl, n_out):
    ctrl = np.asarray(ctrl, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    ctrl = np.maximum(ctrl, 1e-8)
    ctrl = ctrl / ctrl.mean()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0,1.0, ctrl.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0,1.0, n_out)
    f = np.interp(x1, x0, ctrl)
    f = np.maximum(f,0.0)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return np.ones(n_out)/n_out
    return f/s
```
`optimize_control_points(ctrl, n_eval, budget_s, name='')`:
```
def optimize_control_points(ctrl, n_eval, budget_s, name=''):
    ctrl=np.asarray(...); ctrl=np.maximum(ctrl,1e-6)
    n_ctrl=ctrl.size
    m=get_fft_len(n_eval)
    p=ctrl_to_p(ctrl,n_eval)
    best=ctrl.copy(); best_score=score_pre(p,m,n_eval)
    sigma=0.12
    deadline=...
    it=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c=best.copy()
       # pick number perturb 1-3
       nconst=int(rng.integers(1, min(4,n_ctrl+1)))
       for _ in range(nconst):
           idx=int(rng.integers(0,n_ctrl))
           c[idx]*=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
           if c[idx] < 1e-8: c[idx]=1e-8
       p_new=ctrl_to_p(c,n_eval)
       sc=score_pre(p_new,m,n_eval)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best=c.copy(); best_score=sc; sigma=min(sigma*1.05, 2.0); no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve+=1; sigma*=0.97
          if no_improve>500:
             # random restart around best? or new random ctrl
             c=best*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.4,n_ctrl)); c=np.maximum(c,1e-8); best=c; no_improve=0; sigma=0.2
       it+=1
       if it%2000==0: print
    return best.copy(), best_score
```

   n=min(p.size,q.size); if p.size!=q.size: return False
   d=np.linalg.norm(p-q); return d < 0.05 * np.sqrt(max(n,1))

Compute h direct:
```
def compute_h_direct(p,w):
    n=p.size; h=np.zeros(n)
    # Could vectorize via np.correlate? h_i = dot(p, w[i:i+n]). This is cross-correlation. Use loops.
    for i in range(n):
        h[i] = np.dot(p, w[i:i+n])
    return h
```
For n=64, 64 dots length up to64, fine. For n=128, 128*128=16k. OK. For n=256, 65k. Good. For 1000 iters n=128, 6.5M ops, fine.

Implement DE safely:
```
def run_de(n, bounds=(0,1), popsize=15, maxiter=80, tol=1e-6):
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    res = differential_evolution(obj_de, bounds=[(0,1)]*n, popsize=popsize, maxiter=maxiter, seed=1, tol=tol, atol=0, polish=False, init='sobol')
```

Let's write `score_seq(p)` direct/FFT:
```
def score_seq(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    p = np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    p=p/s
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf
    if n <= 128:
       conv=np.convolve(p,p)
       maxq=float(conv.max())
    else:
       m=get_fft_len(n)
       fut=np.fft.rfft(p,m); q=np.fft.irfft(fut*fut,m); maxq=float(q[:2*n-1].max())
    if not finite or maxq<0: maxq=0
    return float(2*n*maxq)
```
`conv_square_seq(p)` returns q, n:
```
if n<=128: conv=np.convolve(p,p); return conv, n
else FFT q...
```

```
n=p.size
if n<=256:
   h=np.empty(n)
   # Optimize by using np.correlate? w length 2n-1. h_i = sum p_j w_{i+j}. This is correlation with positive lags. We can compute via convolution with reversed p maybe direct O(n^2) loop.
   # Use np.einsum? h = np.einsum('j,ij->i', p, w_shift) but w_shift shape n,n.
   for i in range(n): h[i]=np.dot(p,w[i:i+n])
else:
   m=get_fft_len(n); ... as before
```

Could implement a simple hill-climbing:
```
def coordinate_polish(p, budget):
   best=p; best_score=score_seq(p)
   while time:
      moved=False
      for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):
          if i==j: continue
          for frac in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8]:
             amount=best[i]*frac
             q=best.copy(); q[i]-=amount; q[j]+=amount; q[i]=max; q/=sum; sc=score_seq(q)
             if sc<best_score: accept
      if not moved: break
```
O(n^2*iters) but n small. This can polish DE/LP results. However mirror gradient may be better. We can include coordinate for final best if n≤32 and time left.

def make_two_step_length(n, split=0.5, ratio=2.0):
    if n<=1: return [1.0]/n
    k=int(round(n*split)); k=min(max(1,n-1),k)
    low = 1.0 / (1.0 + ratio*(n-k)/k)
    high = ratio*low
    p=np.empty(n); p[:k]=low; p[k:]=high; p/=p.sum(); return p
```

`run_de(n, budget, seed)`:
```
def run_de(n, budget_s, seed=1):
    if not HAVE_DE: return None
    start=...
    try:
       bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
       res = differential_evolution(de_objective, bounds, popsize=15, maxiter=100, seed=seed, tol=1e-8, atol=0.0, polish=False, init='sobol', updating='deferred', workers=1)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res.success and res.x is not None:
       p=normalize_nonnegative(res.x)
       return p
    return None
```

Now, small integer search: Need import itertools. Top-level.
```
def integer_search(n, max_w, include_zero, max_evals, deadline):
   values = list(range(0 if include_zero else 1, max_w+1))
   best=None; best_score=np.inf
   count=0
   for tup in itertools.product(values, repeat=n):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       arr=np.array(tup, dtype=float)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s <=0: continue
       p=arr/s
       sc=score_seq(p)
       if sc<best_score:
          best=p; best_score=sc
       count+=1
       if count>=max_evals: break
   return best,best_score
```
Product from 0 for n=10 W=3 => 4^10 1,048,576; max_evals maybe 200k. Good.

We can run integer search with budgets:
- n=4..8, max_w=4 no zeros: 4^8=65536 total maybe 87k, fine.
- n=5..10, max_w=3 with zeros: up to 1M but max_evals 120k each? That's huge 7*120k=840k, too. Use total budget 180s. For n=5..10, W=3 include_zero, max_evals per n: [5000,15000,30000,50000,80000,120000]? Sum ~300k. Each score small. OK. Values 0..3. Could miss but random/DE cover.

```
def make_random_length(n, rng):
    x = rng.exponential(1.0, size=n) + 0.05

```
def lp_polish(p_init, budget_s, max_iters=30):
    if not HAVE_LINPROG: return p_init, score_seq(p_init)
    p=normalize_nonnegative(p_init)
    n=p.size
    if n>128 or n<3: return p,score_seq(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_seq(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    c=-np.ones(n)
    for _ in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       q,M=conv_square_seq(p)
       M_raw=M*n*n
       f=p*n
       A=build_conv_matrix_dense(f)
       b=np.full(2*n-1, M_raw)
       try: res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs')
       except: break
       if not res.success: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
       g=np.maximum(g,0)
       sum_g=float(g.sum())
       if sum_g <= n*(1+1e-6): break
       g = g * (n/sum_g)
       improved=False
       for t in (0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1):
          f_new=f + t*(g-f) # same
          f_new=np.maximum(f_new,0)
          s=f_new.sum(); if s<=: continue
          p_new=f_new/s
          sc=score_seq(p_new)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
             p=p_new.copy(); best=p_new.copy(); best_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved: break
    return best, best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for low-score non-negative step-function coefficients.

The program searches small-to-medium lengths first, using:
  - exact direct convolution for short sequences,
  - integer / Sobol / DE-style global starts,
  - linear-programming "best response" polishing for short lengths,
  - multiplicative gradient polishing with a smooth max active-lag weight,
  - final polishing of the best sequence found.

The returned sequence is scaled so that it is far from the evaluator's
sum-positivity and clipping boundaries while preserving the score
(scale invariance).
"""

import time
import math
import itertools
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    HAVE_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_LINPROG = False

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    HAVE_DE = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_DE = False

try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len
    HAVE_NEXT_FAST = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_NEXT_FAST = False

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
DIRECT_CONV_LIMIT = 96
DIRECT_H_LIMIT = 256
rng_global = np.random.default_rng(20240527)


def get_fft_len(n):
    L = max(1, 2 * int(n) - 1)
    if HAVE_NEXT_FAST:
        try:
            return int(next_fast_len(L))
        except Exception:
            pass
    if L <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    x = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if x.size == 0 or s <= 1e-14:
        n = max(1, int(x.size))
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return x / s


def score_seq(p):
    """Scale-invariant exact/Fast score for a non-negative sequence."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if p.size == 0 or s <= 1e-14:
        return np.inf
    p = p / s
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= DIRECT_CONV_LIMIT:
        conv = np.convolve(p, p)
        maxq = float(conv.max())
    else:
        m = get_fft_len(n)
        fut = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fut * fut, m)
        maxq = float(conv[:2 * n - 1].max())
    if not np.isfinite(maxq) or maxq < 0.0:
        maxq = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * maxq)


def conv_square_seq(p):
    """Return full linear convolution square and length n."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= DIRECT_CONV_LIMIT:
        q = np.convolve(p, p)
        return q, n
    m = get_fft_len(n)
    fut = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    q = np.fft.irfft(fut * fut, m)
    return q[:2 * n - 1], n


def smooth_weights(q, beta):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if q.size == 0:
        return q.copy()
    M = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.full(q.size, 1.0 / q.size, dtype=np.float64)
    shifted = beta * (q - M)
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(q.size, 1.0 / q.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / ws
    return w


def compute_h_seq(p, w):
    """
    h_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}.
    Direct for short sequences, FFT otherwise.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= DIRECT_H_LIMIT:
        h = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            h[i] = float(np.dot(p, w[i:i + n]))
        return np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    m = get_fft_len(n)
    pr = p[::-1]
    hp = np.fft.rfft(pr, m)
    hw = np.fft.rfft(w, m)
    hfull = np.fft.irfft(hp * hw, m)
    h = hfull[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(h, 0.0)


def polish_sequence(p, budget_s, beta=90.0, name=""):
    """
    Multiplicative gradient polishing using a smooth maximum of the
    convolution-square coefficients.  Keeps the best valid sequence.
    """
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return p, np.inf

    floor = 1e-12
    p = (1.0 - floor) * p + (floor / n)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = score_seq(best)
    cur = p.copy()
    eta = 0.38
    no_improve = 0
    restarts = 0
    it = 0

    if budget_s <= 1e-9:
        return best, best_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q, _ = conv_square_seq(cur)
        M = float(q.max()) if q.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        score = 2.0 * n * M

        if score < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = score
            best = cur.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        w = smooth_weights(q, beta)
        h = compute_h_seq(cur, w)
        hm = float(h.mean())
        hs = float(h.std())
        if not np.isfinite(hs) or hs <= 1e-14:
            h_unit = np.zeros_like(h)
        else:
            h_unit = (h - hm) / hs

        improved = False
        etas = (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta) if no_improve > 3 else (eta,)
        old = cur.copy()
        for e in etas:
            p_new = cur * np.exp(-e * h_unit)
            p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 1e-15)
            pn = float(p_new.sum())
            if pn <= 1e-14:
                continue
            p_new = p_new / pn
            sc = score_seq(p_new)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-15:
                cur = p_new.copy()
                if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                    best = cur.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                eta = min(max(e, 0.02), 1.35) * 1.04
                improved = True
                no_improve = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            eta *= 0.82
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 28:
            restarts += 1
            noise = 0.09 if restarts <= 5 else 0.18
            cur = best * (1.0 - noise) + noise * rng_global.random(n)
            cur = np.maximum(cur, 1e-15)
            cur = cur / cur.sum()
            no_improve = 0
            eta = 0.42
            if restarts > 14:
                break

        it += 1
        if it % 200 == 0:
            print(f"    polish[{name}] iter={it} best={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    return best.copy(), float(best_score)


def build_conv_matrix_dense(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(f.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(L):
        j0 = max(0, i - n + 1)
        j1 = min(n - 1, i)
        if j1 >= j0:
            A[i, j0:j1 + 1] = f[j0:j1 + 1]
    return A


def lp_polish(p_init, budget_s, max_iters=35):
    """
    Short-length LP best-response polishing.

    Given current f (sum = n), solve max sum(g) s.t. g >= 0 and
    conv(f, g) <= max(conv(f, f)).  If the LP optimum has larger sum,
    mix toward it and accept only if the score improves.
    """
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p_init)
    n = int(p.size)
    base_score = score_seq(p)
    if not HAVE_LINPROG or n < 3 or n > 128 or budget_s <= 1e-9:
        return p, base_score

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = base_score
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        q, _ = conv_square_seq(p)
        M = float(q.max()) if q.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        M_raw = M * n * n
        f = p * n
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
        A = build_conv_matrix_dense(f)
        b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M_raw, dtype=np.float64)

        try:
            res = linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bnds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True}
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        sum_g = float(g.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sum_g) or sum_g <= n * (1.0 + 1e-7):
            break

        g = g * (n / sum_g)
        improved = False
        f_old = f.copy()

        for mix in (0.65, 0.40, 0.22, 0.10):
            f_new = (1.0 - mix) * f_old + mix * g
            f_new = np.maximum(f_new, 0.0)
            sn = float(f_new.sum())
            if sn <= 1e-14:
                continue
            p_new = f_new / sn
            sc = score_seq(p_new)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                p = p_new.copy()
                best = p_new.copy()
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best.copy(), float(best_score)


def integer_search(n, max_w, include_zero, max_evals, deadline=None):
    """Exhaustive-ish integer weight search (scale invariant)."""
    values = list(range(0 if include_zero else 1, max_w + 1))
    best = None
    best_score = np.inf
    count = 0

    for tup in itertools.product(values, repeat=n):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        if count >= max_evals:
            break

        arr = np.array(tup, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            count += 1
            continue
        p = arr / s
        sc = score_seq(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best = p.copy()
        count += 1

    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_score = score_seq(best)
    return best, float(best_score)


def run_de(n, seed=1, maxiter=70, popsize=15):
    """Small-n differential evolution over normalized non-negative variables."""
    if not HAVE_DE or n < 3:
        return None
    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        init = "sobol"
        try:
            res = differential_evolution(
                de_objective,
                bounds,
                popsize=popsize,
                maxiter=maxiter,
                seed=seed,
                tol=1e-8,
                atol=0.0,
                polish=False,
                init=init,
                updating="deferred",
                workers=1
            )
        except Exception:
            res = differential_evolution(
                de_objective,
                bounds,
                popsize=max(10, popsize),
                maxiter=max(30, maxiter // 2),
                seed=seed,
                tol=1e-7,
                polish=False,
                workers=1
            )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and res.x is not None:
            p = normalize_nonnegative(res.x)
            return p
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def de_objective(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if x.size == 0 or s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e6
    p = x / s
    sc = score_seq(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e6
    return float(sc)


def make_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(max(1, int(n)), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, int(n))


def make_two_step(n, split=0.5, ratio=2.0):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    k = int(round(n * split))
    k = min(max(1, k), n - 1)
    ratio = max(1e-6, float(ratio))
    low = 1.0 / (1.0 + ratio * float(n - k) / float(k))
    high = ratio * low
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:k] = low
    p[k:] = high
    return normalize_nonnegative(p)


def make_ramp(n, alpha=1.0):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f = 1.0 + float(alpha) * (x - 0.5)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-6)
    return normalize_nonnegative(f)


def make_random_length(n, rng):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    f = 0.2 + np.abs(z) + 0.25 * np.clip(z, 0.0, None)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-6)
    return normalize_nonnegative(f)


def interpolate_to(arr, n_out):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return make_uniform(n_out)
    if arr.size == 1:
        return np.full(int(n_out), float(arr[0]))
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_out))
    y = np.interp(x1, x0, arr)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonnegative(y)


def tuple_score(item):
    return float(item[0])


def prepare_output_sequence(seq):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, None)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    n = int(seq.size)
    if n <= 0 or s <= 1e-12:
        seq = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 16.0
        n = 16

    target_sum = max(1.0, float(n))
    maxv = float(seq.max())
    scale = target_sum / s
    if maxv > 0.0:
        scale = min(scale, 1000.0 / maxv)
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
        scale = 1.0
    seq = seq * scale
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    fs = float(seq.sum())
    if fs < 0.01:
        seq = np.full(n, 0.01 / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        fs = 0.01

    return [float(x) for x in seq]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Top-level search invoked by the evaluator harness.
    """
    global rng_global

    rng_global = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 20240527)

    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 0.0:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    deadline = start + max(0.0, total_budget - 25.0)

    print(f"Propose start: budget={total_budget:.1f}s, target={TARGET_SCORE}", flush=True)

    best_seq = make_uniform(16)
    best_score = score_seq(best_seq)
    length_best_score = {}
    length_best_seq = {}

    def consider(seq):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if seq is None:
            return
        p = normalize_nonnegative(seq)
        sc = score_seq(p)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        n = int(p.size)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = p.copy()
            print(f"NEW BEST: n={n} score={sc:.7f}", flush=True)
        if (n not in length_best_score) or (sc < length_best_score[n] - 1e-14):
            length_best_score[n] = sc
            length_best_seq[n] = p.copy()

    # Use any provided prior construction if available.
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    hs_arr = None
    if hs is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(hs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
            if arr.size > 0 and arr.sum() > 0.0:
                hs_arr = arr
                consider(hs_arr)
        except Exception:
            hs_arr = None

    # 1) Integer searches: very effective for short lengths.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        print("Phase 1a: positive integer searches", flush=True)
        for n in range(4, 9):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            max_evals = 120000
            if n == 8:
                max_evals = 70000
            p, _ = integer_search(n, 4, False, max_evals, deadline)
            consider(p)

        print("Phase 1b: integer searches allowing zeros", flush=True)
        zero_plan = [
            (5, 25000),
            (6, 45000),
            (7, 70000),
            (8, 120000),
            (9, 100000),
            (10, 70000),
        ]
        for n, maxev in zero_plan:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                break
            p, _ = integer_search(n, 3, True, maxev, deadline)
            consider(p)

    # 2) Random / parametric starts plus short polishing across lengths.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        print("Phase 2: fast scan over lengths with multiplicative polish", flush=True)

        scan_lengths = [5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48]
        for idx, n in enumerate(scan_lengths):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            left_n = max(1, len(scan_lengths) - idx)
            per_n = max(8.0, min(22.0, remaining / float(left_n)))

            starts = [
                make_two_step(n, 0.5, 2.0),
                make_ramp(n, 1.2),
                make_random_length(n, rng_global),
            ]
            if hs_arr is not None:
                starts.append(interpolate_to(hs_arr, n))

            per_start = per_n / float(len(starts))
            for st in starts:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
                    break
                pp, sc = polish_sequence(st, per_start, beta=90.0, name=f"scan_n{n}")
                consider(pp)

    # 3) Focus on the best observed lengths.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        print("Phase 3: longer search on promising lengths", flush=True)

        items = [(float(sc), int(n)) for n, sc in length_best_score.items()]
        items.sort(key=tuple_score)

        for idx, (nsc, n) in enumerate(items[:5]):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                break
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            left_items = max(1, min(5, len(items) - idx))
            budget_len = max(25.0, min(220.0, remaining / float(left_items)))

            starts = [
                length_best_seq.get(n, make_two_step(n, 0.5, 2.0)),
                make_random_length(n, rng_global),
            ]
            if hs_arr is not None:
                starts.append(interpolate_to(hs_arr, n))

            per_start = budget_len / float(len(starts))
            for st in starts:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
                    break
                if n <= 128:
                    pll, scp = lp_polish(st, 0.45 * per_start)
                    consider(pll)
                    st = pll
                pp, scp = polish_sequence(st, 0.55 * per_start, beta=110.0, name=f"focus_n{n}")
                consider(pp)

    # 4) Dedicated differential evolution for moderate small n.
    if HAVE_DE and time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        print("Phase 4: differential evolution starts", flush=True)
        de_lengths = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16]
        for n in de_lengths:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40.0:
                break
            for seed in (1, 2, 3):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                p_de = run_de(n, seed=seed, maxiter=65, popsize=15)
                if p_de is not None:
                    consider(p_de)
                    pp, sc = polish_sequence(p_de, 12.0, beta=120.0, name=f"de_n{n}_s{seed}")
                    consider(pp)

    # 5) Final polish of the best known sequence.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 10.0:
        print(f"Phase 5: final polish (remaining={remaining:.1f}s)", flush=True)
        final_budget = min(1200.0, remaining * 0.92)
        pp, sc = polish_sequence(best_seq, final_budget, beta=130.0, name="final")
        consider(pp)

    # Optional very final short restart if budget remains and best is not good enough.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_score > TARGET_SCORE and remaining > 30.0:
        n_try = best_seq.size if best_seq.size else 16
        for k in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
                break
            p_try = make_random_length(n_try, rng_global)
            pp, sc = polish_sequence(p_try, min(90.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.5), beta=130.0, name="late")
            consider(pp)

    out_seq = prepare_output_sequence(best_seq)
    print(f"Internal best: n={best_seq.size if best_seq is not None else 0}, score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    try:
        exact = float(evaluate_sequence(out_seq))
        print(f"Exact evaluator score: {exact:.7f}", flush=True)
        if not np.isfinite(exact):
            out_seq = prepare_output_sequence(make_uniform(16))
            exact2 = float(evaluate_sequence(out_seq))
            print(f"Fallback exact evaluator score: {exact2:.7f}", flush=True)
    except Exception as e:
        print("Exact evaluator check unavailable:", e, flush=True)

    return out_seq
```
